# FleetForge — Current Sessions Queue

**Purpose:** Forward-looking queue of sessions discussed in planning but not yet shipped. Companion to FLEETFORGE_PROGRESS.md (which is the historical archive).

**Discipline:**
- Every session discussed in planning (claude.ai web chat) gets an entry here at the moment it's discussed, not retroactively.
- Each entry includes: session label, scope summary, estimated effort, dependencies, status, source-of-discussion timestamp.
- When a session ships, its entry is marked SHIPPED with the commit refs and date, then archived to PROGRESS.md SESSION LOG. The entry can be removed from this file or kept with SHIPPED tag for a short grace period — operator's call.
- When a session is descoped or superseded, mark DEFERRED or SUPERSEDED with rationale; do not delete silently.
- Update this file in lockstep with FLEETFORGE_PROGRESS.md whenever ship-state changes.
- **Operator commits (D-D136-OPERATOR-COMMIT, locked 2026-05-19):** do not push unrelated changes to main while any session is IN-FLIGHT. Bundling operator work into an IN-FLIGHT session's working tree causes split-commit traceability noise — see D136 operator-commit-rule clause in REFERENCE.md §0 and D-D136-OPERATOR-COMMIT in PROGRESS.md DECISIONS for the canonical workflow (wait, or pause-then-resume with explicit annotation).

---

## Pre-flight check (D136)

Per D136, every Code Desktop session begins with a pre-flight read of this file:

1. Read FLEETFORGE_CURRENT_SESSIONS.md.
2. If any session is marked IN-FLIGHT with Touching domains overlapping the planned scope → halt and surface to operator for direction (serialize and wait, or opt into branch isolation per D136 fallback). IN-FLIGHT-RO sessions never block write sessions; multiple IN-FLIGHT-RO may coexist.
3. If no IN-FLIGHT collision, register the new session entry here with status IN-FLIGHT (write) or IN-FLIGHT-RO (read-only), start timestamp, agent identifier, and Touching domains (write only). Commit the registration as part of the session's first commit, OR as a standalone single-line edit if the operator wants to register before any work.
4. On session end, update the entry to SHIPPED (or DEFERRED / SUPERSEDED as applicable) per existing CURRENT_SESSIONS.md discipline. Remove the IN-FLIGHT multi-line details.

Read-only sessions skip the collision check (step 2) but still register (step 3) for operator visibility.

**Category separation (K-14, locked 2026-05-12 via S-PREDEPLOY-CHECKLIST-CREATE):** This file is the **session queue**. Items that surface during a session and are bug-shaped (a defect in shipped code) file to `FLEETFORGE_PROGRESS.md` → KNOWN ISSUES (`#NNN`). Items that are pre-deploy-shaped (a `.env` key needing a real value, an AWS resource to provision, a DNS record, a smoke/rollback procedure) file to `FLEETFORGE_PREDEPLOY_CHECKLIST.md` in the appropriate category — NOT here, and NOT to KNOWN ISSUES. Filing under the wrong category is documentation divergence (K-12 class).

## Status legend
- **QUEUED** — discussed, prompt drafted (or near-drafted), not yet shipped
- **IN-FLIGHT** — write-mode session currently executing in Code Desktop. Per D136, only one allowed at a time on main. Entry includes start timestamp, agent identifier, and touching domains.
- **IN-FLIGHT-RO** — read-only session (audit, survey, decision-surfacing) running concurrently. Multiple IN-FLIGHT-RO may coexist with one IN-FLIGHT.
- **BLOCKED** — waiting on operator input or upstream session
- **SHIPPED** — landed on origin/main; archive to PROGRESS.md
- **DEFERRED** — scoped out indefinitely with reason
- **SUPERSEDED** — replaced by another session with reason

---

## Active session format

For sessions in IN-FLIGHT or IN-FLIGHT-RO status, render the entry across multiple lines:

**S-EXAMPLE-LABEL** — IN-FLIGHT
  Started: 2026-05-07T14:32 UTC by desktop-1
  Touching: lib/Billing/, app/admin/leases/, FLEETFORGE_DATABASE_MASTER.sql

Required fields: STATUS keyword, Started (ISO 8601 timestamp + operator-supplied agent identifier).
Optional metadata: Touching (file paths or directories the session expects to write — used for collision detection in pre-flight check).

For IN-FLIGHT-RO, the Touching field is not required (read-only doesn't lock):

**S-EXAMPLE-AUDIT** — IN-FLIGHT-RO
  Started: 2026-05-07T14:32 UTC by desktop-2

When the session ships, update the entry to status SHIPPED with commit refs (per existing convention) and remove the multi-line IN-FLIGHT details.

**S-INVOICE-AUDIT-FIX-2** — SHIPPED 2026-06-20 (see PROGRESS.md SESSION LOG row). Closed finding **I14** (`docs/audits/CLAUDE_2026-06-20_invoices.md`), the deferred Mailer-attachment half of S-INVOICE-AUDIT-FIX-1. SES `sendEmail` has no attachment support, so the Compose-Email modal's invoice/document PDFs were logged "attached" but **never delivered** to the customer (the email-history tab lied). FIX (3 files + 1 smoke): (1) new `StorageClient::read(key):?string` (driver-agnostic byte read — local file_get_contents / s3 getObject; null on missing). (2) `Mailer::send()` gains `array $attachments=[]`; `sendViaSes()` branches — no attachments → unchanged plain `sendEmail` (tiny blast radius), attachments → new `sendRawViaSes()` that builds a `multipart/mixed` MIME (nested `multipart/alternative` text+html body + base64 `application/pdf` parts with `Content-Disposition: attachment`) and sends via SES **`sendRawEmail`**. Correctness guards: CR/LF-stripped headers (injection defense), RFC 2047 subject/name + RFC 2231 filename encoding, base64 + `chunk_split(76)` (≤998-char lines), random boundaries, 10 MB cap. (3) `EmailService::send()` resolves each persisted attachment's storage key → bytes via `StorageClient::read()` and threads `[{content,name,type}]` into Mailer; **fail-closed** if an attachment can't be read (send fails rather than deliver a false "attached"). Only the Compose modal passes attachments (invoice send.php → pdf_path/file_path); dunning emails the HTML body only (its PDF is archived, not attached — separate I15). TESTS: new `tests/_smoke_mailer_attachments.php` 22/22 (read round-trip, MIME structure, base64→exact-bytes, CRLF-can't-inject, RFC 2047/2231, ≤998 lines, EmailService threading + fail-closed); `_smoke_portal_invite_email` 5/5, `_smoke_portal_user_dup_email` 2/2, `_verify_email_templates_redesign` 43/43 (positional callers unaffected by the new trailing param). php -l clean (4); doc_freshness 29/29. No schema change. **DEFERRED → F36**: operator live-SES verify (send a real Compose-modal invoice email, confirm the PDF arrives + renders; prod SES sandbox/read-only for the agent). Next module audited = equipment.

AND status='pending'`, rowCount===1) with release-on-failure across all 3 branches. Verified by NEW `tests/_smoke_ai_medium_fixes.php` 14/14 + 85/85 write smoke + parity/reproduce-master/doc_freshness green. **Follow-up 2 (same day): NOT-NULL-date MEDIUM + 2 high-value LOWs fixed** — (a) `validateField` rejects clearing a required date (`required:true`) instead of 1048→stuck proposal; (b) `insights.php` now selects the real ai_anomaly_alerts columns (alert_type/title/description, not category/summary) so the Insights anomaly panel isn't permanently empty (+ narrowed catch); (c) `summary-stream.php` now reads the cache via shared `SummaryEngine::cachedSummary()` on force=0 (skips the paid call) and the card's "Cached result" badge works — `ai.cache_summaries` is now functional. **S-CARTAGE-VOID-REBILL** — SHIPPED 2026-06-19 (see PROGRESS.md SESSION LOG row). End-to-end dev check of the 4 service charges found Cartage silently lost when a lease is returned within its first billing period: activation's partial_start invoice carries cartage, the close overshoot-reconciliation voids+reissues it, and the `cartage_billed_at` once-guard left cartage stranded on the void invoice (customer undercharged). Sweep/Wash/Fuel were fine. Fix (operator chose live-aware guard, no schema change): `InvoiceGenerator` emits cartage only when no NON-VOID invoice already has a cartage line (FOR UPDATE retained for no-double-bill; `cartage_billed_at` demoted to display/edit-lock). Verified e2e post-fix: cartage re-bills on the live reissue (`closeout_charges` all four live). `_smoke_lease_service_charges.php` +T2b (cartage re-bills after carrier void) — 5/5; regression green.

**S-LEASE-CREATE-ENDPOINT-SMOKE** — SHIPPED 2026-06-19 (see PROGRESS.md SESSION LOG row). Closes the test gap that let the lease-create 500 (use()-closure capture trap, FLEETFORGE-W / a6115d3) reach prod: feature smokes only call `InvoiceGenerator::createFromLease()` directly, never the `create.php` endpoint whose db_insert runs inside `function () use (...)`. NEW `tests/_smoke_lease_create_endpoint.php` drives a fully valid create through the REAL endpoint (subprocess + php://input wrapper + in-process super_admin session) so the insert closure executes; T1 asserts the new columns (mileage_tracking_mode/engine_hours_at_start/cartage_amount) all persist (NULL/500 if a var is missing from use()), T2 minimal create defaults mode='off'. Proven non-vacuous (dropping $cartageAmount from use() → T1 fails, restore → passes). No code/schema change — create.php already fixed in a6115d3; this is the missing CI guard.

**S-ODO-VALIDATION** — SHIPPED 2026-06-19 (see PROGRESS.md SESSION LOG row). Bugfix: completed lease MTTS82 showed Starting Odometer (17,112.59) ABOVE Latest Recorded (12,198.00) with Total KM Driven clamped to 0.00. Root cause (confirmed on prod): a back-dated lease (start_date 2025-07-26, activated+closed 2026-06-18) captured the trailer's CURRENT GPS odometer as its STARTING reading; the manual closing reading (the real earlier return value) came in below it; no end≥start validation + the `max(0,…)` distance clamps hid the impossible negative. Fix: **Samsara pull gated SOLELY by the mileage_tracking_mode toggle** — NO back-dating heuristic (operator-corrected; a first pass added a 7-day skip, removed — backfilling 7d or 7y is irrelevant, set Manual for historical leases). `close.php` rejects (422) a closing odometer below the start before invoice gen (pure data integrity — the real recurrence guard); close modal warns client-side. NEW `tests/_smoke_lease_odometer_close_guard.php` 2/2 incl. a reusable detector query — run read-only on prod it flagged **2 records to correct: MTTS82 + MTTS11** (both gps-sourced starts; operator fixes, agent is prod-read-only). No schema change; close/activation regression green.

**S-LEASE-SERVICE-CHARGES** — SHIPPED 2026-06-19 (see PROGRESS.md SESSION LOG row). One-time lease service charges. **Cartage** (delivery) entered at lease creation, manual $ no default, bills ONCE on the first invoice (`leases.cartage_amount` + `cartage_billed_at` FOR-UPDATE once-guard in InvoiceGenerator). **Sweep / Wash / Fuel** entered in the close modal — sweep/wash flat (defaults $30/$120), fuel = gallons × per-gallon rate (default $13/gal, server-authoritative); each optional/skippable, pre-filled from `settings` group `billing`. Closeout charges ride on the rental final invoice via `extra_lines`; a post-pass emits a standalone base-rental-free `adjustment` invoice when no final invoice is generated, so charges (and any mileage overage) never silently drop. Migration 202606190600 (item_type ENUM += cartage/sweep/wash/fuel; 2 lease cols; 3 billing settings). New `tests/_smoke_lease_service_charges.php` 3/3; billing/lease regression green; parity/reproduce-master/doc_freshness green; php -l 6/6. Migration operator-applied; defaults tunable in Settings→Billing.

Captured at create (Starting Engine Hours when rate set), close (Current Engine Hours → bills remaining on partial_end final invoice + sets engine_hours_at_end), and manual invoice creation (per-period). Edit/show wired (start whitelisted on active leases via non-posted `_hourlyRate` gate). Unattended monthly cron does NOT bill hours (manual-only). NEW `tests/_smoke_lease_hourly_billing.php` 4/4; 17/17 regression green; parity/reproduce-master/doc_freshness green; UI verified in preview; php -l 10/10. **S-LEASE-MIN-DAYS** — SHIPPED 2026-06-19 (see PROGRESS.md SESSION LOG row). Short-lease billing floor: when a lease's total billable days (start→extent) < N, both engines bill a flat N × daily_rate instead of the actual short duration (default N=3, mainly chassis). 3 config layers — `rate_card_items.minimum_days` > `leases.minimum_billing_days` (frozen at creation, operator-overridable) > `settings lease.minimum_billing_days='3'`. Floor in `HolisticLeaseEngine::cumulativeCorrect` + `ProRateCalculator::calculate` (both: minDays>=2 AND days<minDays AND daily>0 → flat, label `daily_minimum`); threaded via `InvoiceGenerator`. Migration 202606190400 (2 cols + settings seed + chassis rate-card seed=3). UI: lease create/edit/show, rate-card create/show + APIs, Settings→Billing, lookup_rates. Backfill `scripts/backfill_minimum_days_chassis_leases.php` (dry-run default; active chassis only). **Adversarial verify caught a CRITICAL fatal:** `daily_minimum` isn't a `rate_method` ENUM member → 1265 truncation aborted close on legacy `period_independent` leases; clamped to `'daily'` at both write sites + `mapTierToRateMethod` extended. NEW `tests/_smoke_lease_minimum_days.php` 34/34 (incl. T7 legacy end-to-end regression guard); php -l 18/18; doc_freshness 29/29. **Master parity RED on this commit by design:** commit contains ONLY the 2 min-days columns; the workflow also swept up `ai_pending_changes` (S-AI-WRITE-1) + `mileage_tracking_mode` (S-LEASE-MILEAGE-MODE) drift which was excluded per operator (operator reconciles that pre-existing drift). **S-FORM-DRAFT-ROLLOUT** — SHIPPED 2026-06-16 (see PROGRESS.md SESSION LOG row). **Rolled out the existing `FF_FormDraft` autosave helper (REUSED, not modified — `app.js` untouched) to 20 substantial multi-field create/edit pages beyond lease.** Each opted in via `FF_FormDraft.attach({formId, entityId, el:this.$root, model:this.form, version:'1', exclude:[…]})` in `init()` + `this._draft.clear(true)` on the confirmed-success path. **Opted in (20):** equipment templates create+edit, equipment units create+edit, customers create+edit, invoices create, vendors create+edit, reservations create, damage_claims create, inspections create, maintenance_work_orders create, rates create, credit_notes create, payments create, accounting/budgets create+edit, accounting/recurring-entries create, users create. **Excludes:** edit forms with a lock token → `['id','updated_at']` (equipment-unit-edit, customer-edit, budgets-edit); FF_RecordPicker ids (customer_id/lease_id/equipment_unit_id/vendor_id/invoice_id/source_*) — can't rehydrate; transient odometer GPS metadata (invoices). Payments drafts amount/reference/method-type (no card/bank credential fields exist; `card_last_four`=last4 only). **Skipped (documented):** accounting index-page modals (JE/bills/COA/tax/categorization-rules — restore-banner-on-page-load needs modal-aware support = a helper change, out of reuse-only scope), report generators (balance-sheet/cash-flow/P&L/asset-schedule — not create/edit), auth/password + portal credit-application forms (credentials/sensitive, different layout), yards/users/profile index+show modals, `mileage_logs/create` (vanilla-JS form, no Alpine model). **Fixed 4 agent-introduced bugs before commit:** clear() was ungated in `.then()`/await handlers (vendors create/edit, credit_notes, users) where `FF_Api.post` resolves with `{success:false}` on 422 → would wipe the draft on a FAILED save; gated all on the success flag. SC1 php -l 20/20 + node --check (app.js unchanged); SC2–SC6 verified on the REAL authenticated pages (equipment-unit-edit, equipment-template-create, customer-edit, invoice-create, payment-create) — attach binds, banner+Restore rehydrate, excludes never written, real save clears draft + redirects (DB unchanged); SC7 saves unaffected (additive; endpoints/payload/lock untouched); SC8 skips verified (0 autosave in skipped files). **S-FORM-DRAFT-AUTOSAVE** — SHIPPED 2026-06-16 (see PROGRESS.md SESSION LOG row). **Reusable form-draft autosave helper — never lose multi-section edits on Back/refresh/close.** NEW `window.FF_FormDraft` (public/assets/js/app.js §07h): debounced (500ms) mirror of a form's reactive model to `localStorage` under `ff_draft:<formId>:<entityId>`, payload `{v:DRAFT_VERSION, t, s:{<section>:{d,t}}}` (versioned + timestamped + section-sliced for multi-page wizards). SC1 php -l 2/2 + node --check; SC2–SC8 verified against the REAL app.js via a standalone Alpine harness (0 console errors) — surfaced+fixed a double-init duplicate-banner bug; SC9 lock untouched; doc_freshness 29/29. **S-FIX-PORTAL-FORGOT-EMAIL** — SHIPPED 2026-06-07 (see PROGRESS.md SESSION LOG row). **Follow-up to S-FIX-PORTAL-INVITE-EMAIL — fixes the IDENTICAL never-wired email stub in the PUBLIC portal "Forgot password" flow (`app/portal/auth/forgot_password.php`).** It wrote the reset link to `logs/mail.log` via `file_put_contents` ("dev mode — no SES configured") and never called the Mailer → portal users self-serving a password reset got NO email in prod. Fix mirrors `reset_password.php`: `use Mailer/EmailService`, replaced the stub with a branded `EmailService::renderEmailHtml()` body + `Mailer::send()` (SES in prod / mail.log in dev). The send stays INSIDE the existing `if ($user)` block, so the page still shows the same generic success message either way — email-enumeration-safe; `Mailer::send` returns false (never throws) on a bad/disabled address so it can't reveal account existence or break the page. Extended `tests/_smoke_portal_invite_email.php` to 5/5 (NEW C5: forgot_password.php uses Mailer, no mail.log stub). Deprecated `settings/portal_users.php` left untouched per note. DEPLOY PENDING. **S-FIX-PORTAL-INVITE-EMAIL** — SHIPPED 2026-06-07 (see PROGRESS.md SESSION LOG row). **Portal-user INVITE + admin "Send Reset Link" produced no email in prod — root cause: never-wired STUB, NOT SES/sandbox and NOT an APP_ENV mis-set.** Both live admin endpoints (`api/v1/portal_users/create.php`, `reset_password.php`) wrote the link to `logs/mail.log` via `file_put_contents` unconditionally and NEVER called the Mailer (create.php literally: "Dev-mode mail-log entry. Production wires a real mailer here."). Proven on prod: `APP_ENV=production` + AWS creds present + region us-west-2 (correct), yet the recent invite to nandaarvind98@yahoo.com (01:43) sat in prod `logs/mail.log` → the stub branch was taken, not SES. Fix (Path A): both endpoints now build a branded `EmailService::renderEmailHtml()` body and call `Mailer::send()`, which auto-routes SES in prod / mail.log in dev (`isLogMode()===false` when `APP_ENV==='production'`). Endpoints return `email_sent` + an env-accurate `message`; the two UI sites (`users/index.php`, `portal_users/show.php`) now show that message + the static "written to mail.log" copy is env-gated. **S-AI-EMAIL-DIAGNOSE-2** — SHIPPED (diagnostic + prod cleanup; no repo code changed)
  Completed 2026-06-07. **CORRECTS DIAGNOSE-1's false root cause.** Crontab is NOT empty — it's installed under **www-data** (`sudo -u www-data crontab -l`), not ubuntu; DIAGNOSE-1 checked the wrong user. `notification_digest.php` runs hourly, `ai_fleet_brief.php` 04:00 (generating briefs daily — confirmed in cron.log), `ai_anomaly_scan.php` 04:30. **REAL ROOT CAUSE: SQL parameter-order bug in `cron/notification_digest.php` run_morning_digest_emails() (~lines 231–253).** `$sqlParams = $roles` puts roles first, but the SQL has the 3 hour-gate placeholders BEFORE `ur.slug IN (?)`. So `'super_admin'` binds to `briefing_hour=?` and integer `7` binds to `ur.slug IN (?)` → `ur.slug IN (7)` matches no role → **0 recipients every hourly run** (cron.log: `emails(sent=0 skipped=0 errors=0)` forever; notification_log morning_digest rows EVER = 0). Force path works (hourGate=`1=1`, 0 placeholders → roles bind correctly) which masked it in DIAGNOSE-1. PROVEN on prod: buggy order @hour7=0 recipients, corrected order @hour7=3 (Avi, SC8 Test Admin, SC8 MFA Test). SES delivery confirmed (targeted send to Avi → TRUE). CLEANUP done: /tmp/ff_diag_*.php deleted; logs dir 755→775 www-data:www-data (cron+fpm both www-data; perm warning was an artifact of ubuntu manual runs — run diagnostics as `sudo -u www-data`). Secondary: `ai_weekly_brief.php` MISSING from crontab + 0 weekly_brief_opt_in users; `ai.briefing_recipient_roles=["super_admin"]` excludes 10 opted-in mgr/acct users. Fix session: **S-AI-DIGEST-PARAM-FIX** (reorder params; add smoke that runs the NON-force path).

SES confirmed working (Mailer::send → TRUE, test brief delivered to nandaarvind98@yahoo.com). Fix session: S-AI-EMAIL-FIX (install crons + fix log perms + confirm recipient roles).

Worker bugs fixed: Bug A (`$completed++` fired unconditionally; fix: only on success), Bug B (queue row always set status='completed'; fix: inspect `$result['success']` → completed or failed+error_message), Bug C (`acc_qbo_sync_log` had null queue_id/entity_id; fix: `QuickBooksClient::setWorkerContext()` static pattern + `writeSyncLog()` fallback). **S-QBO-FIXPACK-2** — SHIPPED 2026-05-25 (see PROGRESS.md SESSION LOG row). **CustomerPusher + VendorPusher CurrencyRef emission (sibling to FIXPACK-1 InvoicePusher fix).** Operator-discovered live that FF CAD customer #1 (LP Logistics Inc.) was pushed to QBO sandbox and recorded with CurrencyRef=USD because CustomerPusher never emitted CurrencyRef; QBO defaulted to home currency (USD in Intuit US sandbox). Intuit forbids currency change after creation — mapping permanently poisoned. D-QBO-FIXPACK-6 establishes class-of-entity principle (every Pusher for currency-aware QBO entities MUST emit CurrencyRef on create). CustomerPusher: emits CurrencyRef from customers.currency on create; throws QuickBooksException on empty/null currency; strips CurrencyRef from update payloads. VendorPusher: hardcodes 'CAD' (D-QBO-FIXPACK-8 Option A; vendors table has no currency column; backlog S-VENDOR-CURRENCY-COLUMN). Both: D-QBO-FIXPACK-10 idempotency-replay mismatch warning — when already_mapped is detected, probes QBO entity CurrencyRef and error_logs if currency doesn't match expected; catches \Throwable so offline smokes still pass. Smoke gate: _smoke_qbo_customer_push 10→13 sub-checks (C11 CAD emit, C12 USD lowercase strtoupper, C13 empty throws); _smoke_qbo_vendor_push 10→12 sub-checks (C11 hardcoded CAD, C12 documents FIXPACK-8 Option A — passed 'USD' key still emits 'CAD'). **D131 19/19 PASS**. **5 decisions locked** D-QBO-FIXPACK-6 through D-QBO-FIXPACK-10.

**S-QBO-11-FIXPACK-1** — SHIPPED 2026-05-24 (see PROGRESS.md SESSION LOG row). **F2 + F3 fixpack from S-QBO-11 live verification.** Fix #1 (F2 — blocker): InvoicePusher now always emits CurrencyRef + ExchangeRate (D-QBO-FIXPACK-1/-2); throws `QuickBooksException` on non-CAD invoice with missing/zero exchange_rate_to_cad rather than silently defaulting. Fix #2 (F3): New `QboFieldLimits` constant class + field-length pre-flight gate in `InvoicePreflightGate` catches DocNumber (>21), CustomerMemo (>1000), PrivateNote (>4000), line descriptions (>4000) before wasting HTTP round-trip on QBO error 2050. Currency-mismatch gate (gate 4.5): pre-flight queries QBO customer CurrencyRef and blocks push if FF.currency ≠ QBO.CurrencyRef (typed status `failed_preflight_currency_mismatch`). **Migration 61→62/0/0**: ALTER acc_qbo_invoice_map.push_status ENUM adds `failed_preflight_field_too_long` + `failed_preflight_currency_mismatch`. **5 decisions locked** D-QBO-FIXPACK-1 through D-QBO-FIXPACK-5. **Stop conditions**: customers.currency EXISTS (ENUM CAD/USD); getEntity('customer', $id) EXISTS; callers updated (show.php badge + admin invoices.php filter/retry + retry.php allowlist); PrivateNote typical length ~312 chars (well under 4000). **Admin UI**: 3 files updated for new typed statuses (show.php badge map, quickbooks/invoices.php filter + KPI + retry button, retry.php allowlist). **C34 note**: invoice_line_items.description is varchar(500) — QBO's 4000-char line-description limit is physically unreachable via the DB today; C34 smoke tests the gate logic directly via QboFieldLimits::checkLength offline. **CustomerPusher/VendorPusher**: grep confirms no CurrencyRef emit in their payloads — but Customer/Vendor entities don't carry currency on their own payload (they receive currency from QBO); no fix needed in those pushers. **D131 19/19 PASS**: all 19 smokes + 62/0/0. qbo_invoice_push smoke 29→39 sub-checks.

Migration `202605240000_S-QBO-VALIDATOR-SCOPE-SPLIT.sql` (59→60/0/0): ALTER ADD column + index + backfill of 7 existing critical rows via FF code-pattern CASE. **5 decisions locked**: D-QBO-VALIDATOR-1 (gates by Pusher session), D-QBO-VALIDATOR-2 (critical_category column + 6-category whitelist), D-QBO-VALIDATOR-3 (per-session requirements + undeposited_funds actionable-error policy resolved via AskUserQuestion), D-QBO-VALIDATOR-4 (assertReadyForInvoicePush narrows to ar_clearing+sales_revenue; legacy all-7 moves to assertReadyForFullCompliance), D-QBO-VALIDATOR-5 (exception message names blocking categories + contributing FF accounts). **K-22 via AskUserQuestion** per [[project_qbo_subtype_taxonomy]] + [[feedback_trust_file_over_prompt]] (same K-22 from last session): prompt's FF.acct_subtype='AccountsReceivable' (PascalCase) would never match FF lowercase snake_case; adopted FF code-pattern heuristic instead. **K-22 #2 (silent)**: master_schema_parity required re-ordering the new index in DATABASE_MASTER.sql to match MySQL's ALTER append-at-end behavior. **D131 18/18 PASS** including qbo_account_mapping 23→29 (6 new sub-checks C24-C29). D131 smoke count stays 18. **Backfill verification post-ship**: 0 NULL-category rows among 7 is_critical=1; distribution ar_clearing=1 / ap_clearing=1 / sales_revenue=1 / tax_payable=2 / tax_receivable=2 / undeposited_funds=0 (by design). **S-QBO-11 architectural unblock**: operator only needs to map 1030 AR + 4122 Sales Revenue to QBO; tax/AP/UF accounts can remain unmapped indefinitely.

**S-QBO-12** (Invoice Modification & Void Semantics) — QUEUED. Implements InvoicePusher::pushUpdate (currently a stub returning 'unsupported_in_session') + pushVoid path. UPDATE branch handles QBO Invoice.sparse=true partial updates per QBO API contract; pushVoid posts Invoice.Void operation. SyncToken handshake: load qbo_sync_token from acc_qbo_invoice_map, send with payload, persist returned SyncToken back. Void enqueue fires from api/v1/invoices/void.php (currently no QBO integration). Edge case: void of an invoice that has subsequent payments in QBO — QBO refuses void; surface actionable error to operator. Recommended model: **Sonnet** (smaller scope than S-QBO-11; reuses 80% of S-QBO-11 infrastructure). Recommended chat: continuation OK. **Phase QBO-5 / 1 of N — first FF→QBO invoice push.** Implements the QBO Invoice entity push via the established Pusher/Enqueuer pattern (D-QBO-3-2 + D-PUSHER-CONTRACT) — InvoicePusher creates QBO Invoice entities from FF `invoices` rows on draft→sent transition; InvoiceEnqueuer wires the queue insert from `api/v1/invoices/send.php`. Tax-override pattern per D-QBO-CORE-6 (TxnTaxDetail.TotalTax + TaxCodeRef='NON' using settings.quickbooks.tax_override_code_id auto-wired in S-QBO-9). Calls `AccountValidator::assertReadyForInvoicePush()` (narrowed semantics per D-QBO-VALIDATOR-4) at dispatch top. Engine-version dispatch — period_independent + holistic both push (D-QBO-CORE-12). Line emission consumes `acc_qbo_item_map` (S-QBO-10) including GPS net/gross variant selection per customer's `gps_revenue_presentation` flag (D-QBO-10-2). Recommended model: **Opus**. Recommended chat: **fresh chat** — first invoice push is XL scope (~600 line session prompt) and benefits from dedicated context. Pre-conditions: AR + Sales Revenue mapped to QBO; sandbox connection green; sync_enabled=0 stays during dev (operator flips temporarily for live verification only per D-CPA-5).

**S-QBO-MATCHER-GREEDY-FIX** — SHIPPED 2026-05-24 (see PROGRESS.md SESSION LOG row). **Fixed two bugs in AccountMatcher surfaced during S-QBO-8 live verification (§6.1 + §6.2).** Bug 1 — `findBestMatch` didn't track QBO ids already claimed by an earlier FF iteration; multiple FF accounts could match the same QBO Account, then the auto_match UPDATE loop silently overwrote earlier mappings. Bug 2 — medium-confidence shared-token cascade ignored AccountSubType so `long_term_liability` could token-match QBO `AccountsPayable`. **Fix:** matchAll owns a `$matchedQboIds` set + passes into findBestMatch as `$claimedQboIds`; new `SUBTYPE_EQUIVALENCE` constant gates medium pass on FF (snake_case) → QBO (PascalCase, plural OtherCurrent*) subtype agreement. **Claimed-set ported to all 5 Matchers** (≤5 net lines each per Part C threshold); subtype gate is AccountMatcher-only. **3 decisions locked**: D-QBO-MATCHER-1 (claimed-set tracking), D-QBO-MATCHER-2 (subtype-agreement gate at medium pass; FF snake_case keys per K-22 silent resolution), D-QBO-MATCHER-3 (scope — 5-matcher claimed-set, 1-matcher subtype gate). **K-22 via AskUserQuestion** per [[feedback_trust_file_over_prompt]]: prompt's PascalCase keys + plural-vs-singular QBO subtypes corrected against live FF + QBO data. **D131 18/18 PASS** including qbo_account_mapping 19→23 (4 new sub-checks C20-C23: claimed-set, subtype-gate-rejects, subtype-permissive, SUBTYPE_EQUIVALENCE shape). D131 smoke count stays 18 (no new files). No schema motion, no settings, no migrations. **Live recommendation**: leave existing acc_qbo_account_map alone; operator can re-run auto-match from the UI if desired — auto-derived rows aren't load-bearing because sync_enabled=0 per D-CPA-5.

**S-INTEL-V2** — SHIPPED 2026-05-21 (see PROGRESS.md SESSION LOG row). **12 features across 6 phases expanding the Intelligence tab + AI infrastructure.** Phase A (Token Economics): TokenBudgetMonitor lib + ai_budget_check.php hourly cron + 3 endpoints (token_analytics, ai_request_log, briefing_audit_log) + 3 settings keys (budget_alert_thresholds/last_sent/recipients) + 4 UI cards (usage bar, 4-tile rollup, per-feature breakdown, CSS 7d bar chart, filterable request log, audit feed, alert config). Phase B (Brief Control): generate_brief_now endpoint with 5 cost guardrails + 60min idempotency cache + brief_content endpoint loading by cache_id/date + set_snooze endpoint with shorthand parsers (1d/1w/until_monday/until_DATE) + users.briefing_snoozed_until column + UI Generate-now button + clickable Recent Runs rows opening content modal + Snooze column in recipient table. Phase C (Per-User Customization): users.briefing_hour + users.briefing_sections columns + cron refactor (now runs every hour, filters per-user hour matches; renderBody accepts sections array param filtering 6 section keys) + set_user_preferences endpoint + sections-picker modal + inline hour input per user. Phase D (Multi-Channel): SlackPoster + SmsClient libs (graceful degrade when disabled/no-creds) + users.briefing_channels JSON + slack_user_id + phone_e164 columns + 6 settings keys (slack/twilio enabled + creds) + cron fan-out across email/slack/sms per user + set_channels endpoint with E.164 validation. Phase E (Weekly Digest): users.weekly_brief_opt_in column + 3 ai.weekly_brief_* settings keys + ai_weekly_brief.php cron (day+hour gated + 23h dedup + multi-channel fan-out) + MorningBriefingRenderer::buildWeeklyPayload + renderWeeklyBody. FLEETFORGE_DATABASE_MASTER.sql synced (table inserted between acc_fx_revaluations and acc_lease_classifications); PARITY OK. **Part B** LeaseAmortizationService (~530 lines, all bcmath scale=10 intermediate / scale=2 storage / scale=4 annual rate; no PHP floats): solveImplicitRate (Newton-Raphson with central-difference derivative, MAX_ITER=100, TOL=1e-6, fallback to 6.5% annual on non-convergence; STOP on negative rate); pv private helper (iterative discount-factor accumulation, no bcpow-on-floats, rate=0 degrades to nominal sum); buildSchedule (initial NI = FV for sales_type / FV−IDC for direct_financing, per-period finance=opening×r truncated 2dp + principal=cash−finance + closing=opening−principal); **final-period rounding correction** via prompt's direct formula `principal_corrected = opening_ni − target_closing` (preserves telescoping Σ principal = opening[1] − closing[n] exactly; absorbs the unguaranteed-residual gap into principal[n] for §24.7's residual review); **BPO settlement row** when bargain_purchase_option_amount > 0 (settles from guaranteed residual to 0); generate orchestration (GET_LOCK advisory + classification gate + posted-rows regen guard + bulk INSERT + leases.implicit_rate persist + audit_log); preview (no writes); getSchedule readback. **Part C** 3 endpoints (generate POST / preview GET / show GET — all `journal_entries` permission, InvalidArg→404, Runtime→422) + activate.php hook post-commit non-fatal (attaches `amortization_schedule` block to response). **Part D** app/admin/accounting/leases/show.php (~340 lines + Alpine factory): 2-column header (lease detail + summary card), action bar (Preview → Generate flow; Regenerate super_admin-only blocked on posted rows), 9-col schedule table with Σ tfoot, status badges + JE drill placeholder. Register link swapped to deep-link the new page. **Functional smoke** (`/tmp/lessor2_smoke.php`, live rollback via pre-state snapshot): 5/5 PASS + math invariants — lease #1 reclassified to sales_type (60mo/120mo + $120K FV + 6.5% disc + $10K guar resid + $5K unguar resid + $2.5K IDC + $1 BPO + title_transfers=false). Newton-Raphson converged in <100 iters → monthly 0.0062802495 / annual 7.53%. Schedule: 61 periods (60 regular + 1 BPO), initial NI=$120K, Σ finance=$26,999.62, final closing=$0. Invariants: finance[1]=$753.62 = $120K×monthlyRate (MATCH ✅); telescoping Σ principal[1..61]=$120,000.00 = opening[1]−closing[61] (MATCH ✅, after fixing a sign-flip bug in initial implementation surfaced by the smoke). Idempotency: 2nd generate(regenerate=false) returns same 61 rows. Regenerate(true) with 0 posted: rebuilds cleanly. Rollback: schedule + classification + 3 audit rows deleted; lease back to operating. **D131 6/6 PASS** (PARITY OK + I1-I10 + samsara 16/16 + model_b 20/20 + doc_freshness 17/17 + migrate 39/0/0). Roadmap §11 row 21 flipped 📋 PLANNED → ✅ DONE. Schedule auto-generates at activation only for capital classifications.

Outcome: Phase C-3 CCA Schedule 8 engine + admin UI (spec §23.3, roadmap §11 row 11). **Migration** `202605190500_S-ACCT-CCA-1.sql` (31→32/0/0): NEW TABLE `acc_cca_classes` (9 CRA classes — 8/10/10.1/12/16/50/53/54/55, UNIQUE class_number); NEW TABLE `acc_cca_continuity` (per-fiscal-year per-class T2 Schedule 8 row + is_locked flag); ALTER `acc_fixed_assets` ADD COLUMN cca_class_id + available_for_use_date + is_aiip_eligible + FK. **Seed** `015_acc_cca_classes.sql`: 9 INSERT IGNORE rows; Class 16 ("Mainland primary class") rate=0.40. **4 K-22 prompt-vs-disk divergences resolved** (trust-file-over-prompt): `acquisition_cost` not `original_cost`; `asset_class` not `asset_category`; `proceeds` confirmed (matches spec); no `deleted_at` on acc_fixed_assets (Step 11 uses status<>'disposed'). GVWR proxy = `equipment_units.weight_capacity_lbs` (lbs, threshold 25,990 lbs ≈ 11,788 kg). Legacy `cra_class`/`cra_cca_rate` columns kept alongside new FK — depreciation engine vs CCA engine separation. **Part B** `lib/Accounting/CcaService.php` (NEW ~280 lines): 12-step `compute()` algorithm wrapped in db_transaction + advisory `GET_LOCK('ff_cca_{year}', 10)`; opening UCC from prior-year closing; additions keyed off COALESCE(available_for_use_date, acquisition_date) with fallback_count surfaced in notes; proceeds capped at acquisition_cost per CRA; half_year = 0.5 × max(additions − proceeds, 0) when half_year_rule=1 AND aiip=0 (AIIP short-circuit wired for CCA-2); base = max(ucc_after + aiip − half_year, 0); cca = base × rate; recapture when ucc_after<0; terminal_loss when ucc_after>0 AND class empty AND terminal_loss_applies=1. STOP CONDITION: throws on (cca>0 AND terminal_loss>0). Plus `lock()`, `unlock()`, `getSchedule()`, `classifyGvwrWarning()`. **Part C** 5 NEW endpoints (compute / show / classes / lock / export — CSV in T2 column order + PDF via new `ReportPdfRenderer::ccaSchedule8` landscape A4) + fixed_assets/{create,update}.php UPDATED with cca fields + GVWR warning attached as asset._cca_warning (preserves response shape). All endpoints use `require_permission('journal_entries', view|edit)` per K-22. **Part D** admin pages: cca/index.php with year picker + compute/lock/export controls + 15-col schedule + per-class asset drilldown + always-visible AIIP placeholder banner; fixed-assets/index.php create modal extended with CCA section + GVWR hint; fixed-assets/show.php displays new CCA fields. ### Bug investigation outcomes

**S-COMPOSER-LOCK-FIX** — QUEUED
Scope: The 2026-05-16 production deployment ran `composer update` on the server to resolve PHP 8.2 compatibility (composer.lock had symfony v8.x packages requiring PHP >= 8.4). The updated composer.lock on the server differs from the one in the repo. This session pulls the updated composer.lock from the server and commits it to origin/main so future deployments don't require a manual composer update.
Steps: (1) copy updated composer.lock from server via scp or paste; (2) run `composer install --no-dev` locally to verify it resolves cleanly on PHP 8.2; (3) commit + push.
Effort: ~20 min. 2-commit arc.
Dependencies: none — can ship immediately.
Discovered: 2026-05-16 during Lightsail deployment.

**S-RATE-CARDS-PROD-FIX** — QUEUED
Scope: The Standard 2025 rate card migration (`202605130934_S-SEED-RATE-CARDS-LOAD_standard_2025_default.sql`) fails on a fresh database because it references a `created_by` user ID that doesn't exist yet (FK constraint on `rate_cards.created_by` → `users.id`). Fix: update the migration to use NULL for `created_by` (the column allows NULL per schema — `ON DELETE SET NULL`). Also re-run the rate card seed on production so Standard 2025 rates are available.
Steps: (1) update the migration SQL to use NULL `created_by`; (2) update `schema_migrations` checksum; (3) run the seed on prod manually via mysql; (4) commit fix.
Effort: ~30 min. 3-commit arc.
Dependencies: none — can ship immediately.
Discovered: 2026-05-16 during Lightsail deployment.

**S-AUTH-FIX** — SHIPPED 2026-05-14 (commits cea88fb-e31b25e + this C5 — see PROGRESS.md SESSION LOG)

"Keep me logged in for 30 days" now means no password AND no MFA re-prompt for 30 days. Security gap closed: remember-me with expired MFA restores session at password level but re-gates on MFA (no full logout). Migration 18.

### Strategic / multi-session arcs (planning phase)

**QuickBooks integration** — PLANNING
Scope: bidirectional sync with QBO as source of truth for customers + payments, FleetForge as source for invoices. Implies deprecating most of FleetForge accounting module (S028-S037). Estimated 6-7 sessions: S-QBO-1 discovery, S-QBO-2 customer sync, S-QBO-3 invoice export, S-QBO-4 payment import, S-QBO-5 reconciliation tooling, S-QBO-6 accounting module deprecation, S-QBO-7 cutover.

- **S-CAP-MULTILINE DONE (commit 4464853, 2026-07-09; DEPLOYED)** — manager escalation: prod INV-2026-01760 "all weird and won't let me edit" — stored subtotal **-10.51** with a $0.00 mileage_credit line. The credit-overflow cap capped only the FIRST cappable line then bailed; when the overflow spanned BOTH credit types it left the residual in the subtotal AND over-routed the full abs(subtotal) to the account-credit CN. Fix: drain across all cappable lines, route only what was removed, plus a **hard invariant** (subtotal must be exactly 0.00 post-cap or the txn throws). Regression scenario I added to est-daily smoke (verified fails pre-fix). Suites 547/547 + 18/18 + 81/0 + 30/30.
- **S-ORPHAN-OVERFLOW-CN DONE (commit 810566c, 2026-07-09; DEPLOYED + PROD REMEDIATED)** — root cause #2: deleting/voiding/regenerating an invoice stranded its auto-created overflow CN, which then poisoned later mileage true-ups (orphan CN-CR-2026-00011 $40.01 from deleted INV-2026-01444 halved 01760's credit from $80.68 to $40.67). NEW `lib/Billing/OverflowCreditNotes.php` (findBlockers = applied CN blocks the op; voidForInvoice = auto-void unapplied + issue-JE reversal via NEW `AutoEntryBridge::onCreditNoteVoided`) wired into delete/bulk_delete/voidInvoice/bulk_void/regenerate; also `credit_notes/void.php` now reverses the issue JE (phantom-2060 gap). NEW smoke `_smoke_overflow_cn_lifecycle.php` 8/8 incl. un-poisoned regeneration E2E. **Prod remediation (operator-authorized, dry-run-then-commit, 17 assertions):** CN-11 + CN-12 voided w/ JE reversals; INV-2026-01760 rebuilt in place → **$0.00**, correct capped lines, fresh **CN-CR-2026-00013 $91.19** active (JE balanced); 0 negative-subtotal invoices, 0 orphan CNs, GL 2060 ties to subledger. **2026-06-26:**
- **S-EQTAX (equipment taxonomy) — IN PROGRESS (8-commit arc).** Operator: "this whole category and subcategory thing is very confusing… let's have 2 very clear and separate selectors for category and sub-category — because Combo itself is a type of chassis." Root cause of the "3-day minimum only works sometimes" report (see diagnosis below): `equipment_templates.category` is a flat 1-level enum, so **Combo sat at the SAME level as Chassis** and the chassis-only minimum skipped it. Building a TWO-LEVEL operator-managed taxonomy: **Category → Sub-category**, with billing rules attached at the Category level (Combo → Chassis inherits the minimum). Operator decisions: build all 8 commits incl. a CRUD manage screen (add/edit/remove both levels); keep Combo as its own report line for now (will move it on-screen later). Blast-radius mapped (workflow): ~10 files change, ~40 keep working unchanged via a retained `category` "type slug" mirror.

**2026-06-25:**
- S-SAMSARA-TRAILER-ODO-STALL-FLAG SHIPPED (this commit) — **Flag a trailer's Samsara odometer on the unit page when it reads implausibly low (likely a frozen `gpsOdometerMeters`)** — operator (12TR1303 screenshot): "those units are still showing wrong mileage." The unit shows Mileage 375 km / Live odometer 375.43 km while the Distance Travelled tool (already on the [[S-SAMSARA-TRAILER-GPS-FALLBACK]] GPS fallback) shows 689.81 km moved in June alone. A trailer's odometer is the gateway-derived `gpsOdometerMeters`, frozen on a subset (17 of 165 trailers read nonzero <1000 km); the GPS-fallback fix reached DISTANCE + billing but the headline cumulative odometer the page shows is the stored frozen value, and a true lifetime odometer can't be reconstructed (no history kept). **Operator chose flag-only** (vs an app-maintained GPS odometer or recent-GPS-distance). **Fix (3 files, display-only, no schema/sync/billing change):** new helper `ff_samsara_odometer_likely_stalled()` (trailer + `0<km<floor`; floor = tunable setting `samsara.trailer_odometer_min_plausible_km` default 1000, no migration); `api/v1/equipment/units/show.php` returns a `samsara_odometer_stalled` boolean; `app/admin/equipment/show.php` shows an amber "⚠ odometer may be stalled — see Distance Travelled" on the Mileage card + a ⚠ icon on the Live-Tracking + Samsara-tab odometers. Heuristic catches LOW-frozen values (every reported case); HIGH-frozen needs sync-side odometer-change tracking (offered as a follow-up). Verified: php -l ×3 clean; helper 8/8 (375.43 → flagged, healthy 48000 → no, vehicle → no, edges) + prod-fleet count (17). Needs `ff-deploy`. **Styling follow-up (same day):** the badge initially used `.stat-delta` (which forces `white-space:nowrap`+ellipsis → the message truncated) and the themed `--color-warning-text` (bright #facc15 yellow, low-contrast on the always-cream stat tile in dark mode). Fixed: dropped `.stat-delta`, concise wrapping text ("Odometer may be stalled", full hint in the tooltip), hardcoded `#b45309` amber (reads on the cream/white tile in both themes, matching how the tile hardcodes its dark text colors). Before/after confirmed in a faithful card mock.
- S-LEASE-CONTRACT-NUMBER-EDIT SHIPPED (this commit) — **The contract number is now editable on Edit Lease for pending AND active leases** (operator: "we should be able to edit lease contract number even when the lease is active"). Was read-only. Now `app/admin/leases/edit.php` renders an input bound to `form.contract_number` (dirty-tracked) and `api/v1/leases/update.php` accepts it: required + ≤100 + UNIQUE. The dup pre-check uses `db_count(... WHERE contract_number = ? AND id <> ?)` counting ALL rows (the UNIQUE index is global + spans soft-deleted rows — db_exists would miss a soft-deleted collision and 1062→500), plus a narrow 1062 race catch. No active-status gate needed (ACTIVE_LEASE_DISTANCE_ONLY already gone); completed/cancelled stay immutable. Rename is forward-only — invoices keep `contract_number_snapshot`, everything else keys off lease_id. **Verified live on an active lease (1001723):** rename works + audit-logged; dup → 422 "already in use"; empty → 422 "required"; restored. No schema change. Needs `ff-deploy`.
- S-LEASE-CLOSE-ACTUAL-DATE-ADMIN SHIPPED (this commit) — **Show the FULL rental period (real return date) on the admin invoice surfaces when the return-day-not-billed time-of-day rule trimmed the last billed day** — completes the [[S-LEASE-CLOSE-ACTUAL-DATE]] rollout. **Operator (prod):** lease MTTS110 (start Sep 9 20:00 / return Sep 19 15:00, open-ended) — the rule dropped the return day (15:00 ≤ pickup 20:00) → INV-2026-00250 correctly billed 10 days $171.43, but the invoice LIST showed `2025-09-09 → 2025-09-18`; "the customer should see the full period." The `ff_invoice_display_period_end()` helper already surfaces the actual return for display (while `billing_period_end` stays the trimmed billed extent for coverage/overshoot math) and was wired into the portal list/view + the base_rental line ("…– 2025-09-19 · billed 10 days"), but NOT the admin invoice list/show, the lease- + customer-detail invoice tables, the payment-allocation table, or the portal lease mileage tables. **Fix (7 files, display-only, no billing-math/schema change):** `api/v1/invoices/index.php` returns a `display_period_end` (one endpoint feeds all three admin invoice tables); the three admin JS lists + `invoices/show` + `payments/show` + `portal/leases/view` apply the helper. **Also tightened the helper** (`includes/functions.php`): the old `actual > billed` test mislabeled INTERIM invoices (an August monthly invoice on a Sep-19-returned lease showed "→ Sep 19"); now keys on the exact 1-day-trim signature (`billed + 1 day === actual_return`) so only the final trimmed invoice swaps. Operator "Remove N days" still shows the reduced period by design. **Verified:** php -l ×8 clean; helper 8 scenarios incl. the exact prod row (→ Sep 19) + the interim fix; end-to-end on the real prod join; `_smoke_billing_time_of_day` 35/35. INV-2026-00250 needs no data change — it displays the full period once deployed. Needs `ff-deploy`.

The named-error fix worked (operator's screenshot showed "VIN already in use by unit STL2303") but didn't unblock them — the VIN belongs to STL2303, and clearing it first is painful across many units. Now: NEW `vin_check.php` (GET) powers a live inline note on the unit create+edit forms; NEW `move_vin.php` (POST) atomically clears the VIN off the current holder then sets it on the target (one transaction, clear-then-set so no unique-index 1062, audit-logged, idempotent), surfaced as a "Move VIN to {unit}" button on edit. **Verified end-to-end in the live browser** (typed T5301's VIN on STL2002 → note named T5301 → click Move → DB updated both sides + audit-logged; dev data restored). `_smoke_unit_dupkey_guard.php` SC8/SC8b added → 14/14. - S-SAMSARA-TRAILER-GPS-FALLBACK SHIPPED (this commit) — **Trailers fetching wrong/missing Samsara mileage** (operator: "thousands of km showing as hundreds" + `unit_not_in_samsara`). **Root cause (confirmed live):** affected trailers' `gpsOdometerMeters` is FROZEN (12TR1323 stuck at 145 km since 2025-07-26) while GPS reports fine — and our billing derives trailer distance from the odometer, so it errored or undercounted. **Fix (`SamsaraClient.php`):** new `trailerGpsDistance()` sums the GPS position track (haversine, period-bounded, 15 m jitter floor + 160 km/h teleport filter) as a fallback when a trailer's odometer is absent/no-readings/frozen; prefers the odometer when it's healthy (≥2 in-period readings) and auto-reverts once Samsara reports it again. `source='gps'` (enum-safe). **Validated end-to-end against the live API:** 53D1007 = 2766 km/mo (a real "thousands" trailer), 12TR1323 = 884, 40TR1311 = 285. **2026-06-24:**
- S-HOLISTIC-ALREADY-BILLED-ORDER SHIPPED (this commit) — **Made the holistic running-reconciliation order-independent: regenerating an EARLIER invoice while later ones exist no longer zeroes it.** Root-cause fix for the manual "Regenerate from Lease" footgun. `HolisticLeaseEngine::sumAlreadyBilled` summed EVERY non-void invoice's base with no period filter — fine in-order (later don't exist yet) but on regenerate it counted later invoices, so June with July/Aug present → already_billed exceeded June's cumulative → $0. Fix: `sumAlreadyBilled($leaseId, $throughPeriodEnd)` adds `billing_period_start <= this.period_end`, counting only earlier/overlapping periods; the call passes period_end. In-order generation is byte-identical (filter is a no-op); close/reconciliation still counts all prior+overlapping. **Validated: main runner 547/547 + all billing smokes green; NEW `_smoke_holistic_regenerate_order` 3/3** (regenerate June with July+Aug present keeps $1,500, was $0 pre-fix). Removes the S-INVOICE-LEASE-EDIT-SYNC manual-regenerate caveat. No schema change. Needs `ff-deploy`.
- S-INVOICE-LEASE-EDIT-SYNC SHIPPED (this commit) — **After a lease date change, the edit form now syncs ALL the lease's billing: regenerate changed drafts, GENERATE newly-billable months, VOID drafts the lease no longer covers.** Operator hit "edited the lease, nothing changed" — because extending the END date on a JUNE invoice is a correct no-op, so extending a lease had no visible result. Now `api/v1/leases/update.php` returns three buckets (`affected_drafts` = drafts whose period actually changes, `new_billable_months` = un-invoiced segments via the picker's `ff_billable_months`, `orphaned_drafts` = drafts past the lease end) and `maybeApplyInvoiceChanges` applies them **void → regenerate → generate** in one prompt. **Verified live** (lease June 1→Aug 12): extending the lease → June untouched ($2,240) + July ($2,240) + Aug 1-12 ($896) generated, no $0; shortening → August voided, July clamped. **Key fix:** only regenerate genuinely-changed drafts — regenerating an unchanged earlier invoice while later ones exist zeroes it (holistic `sumAlreadyBilled` counts the later invoices). **Known limitation:** the MANUAL "Regenerate from Lease" button on an early invoice while later ones exist can still churn; proper fix is a period-filtered `already_billed` in the holistic engine (deferred — risk to reconciliation + the 547 suite). No schema change. Needs `ff-deploy`.

Deleted `lib/Billing/ProRateCalculator.php`; collapsed `InvoiceGenerator.php` to holistic-only (removed the engine-version dispatch, the legacy full_month/proRate branches, the legacy-only $0 backstop + base_rental append, the $proRate field, the $engineVersion reads); cleaned legacy cases out of 11 test files (+ deleted the 100%-legacy _stress_billing_rate_exception.php; flipped legacy fixtures in leases.json). Needs `ff-deploy`.
- S-INVOICE-REGEN-PERIOD-CLAMP SHIPPED (this commit) — **Fix: "Regenerate from Lease" now reflects a lease's changed dates.** Operator report: "changed the lease dates, regenerated, the invoice still shows the same date." Cause: regenerate reused the OLD invoice's stored billing period verbatim, so a date change never moved it. (Their specific action — extending the END date past a fully-covered JUNE invoice — is actually correctly a no-op; but moving the start / shortening the lease into the month genuinely wasn't reflected.) Fix (`api/v1/invoices/regenerate.php`): re-derive the period from the lease's current dates — for full_month, compute the NATURAL calendar month first (so a shorten→reextend recovers, instead of monotonically shrinking), then clamp start to max(natural, lease.start) and end to min(natural, extent=actual_return→end_date); 422 PERIOD_OUTSIDE_LEASE if the lease no longer covers it. **Verified live** (INV-2026-00862): extend → June 1-30/$2,240 unchanged; shorten to June 15 → June 1-15/$1,440; re-extend → recovered to June 1-30/$2,240. - S-INVOICE-DRAFT-EDIT (foundation) SHIPPED (this commit) — **Part 1 of editing non-sent (draft) invoices: the authoritative total-recompute helper.** Operator wants to edit everything on a draft AND have lease changes reach linked invoices; design (their calls) = manual line-item editor + regenerate-from-lease (in-place, keep number) + a prompt to regenerate affected drafts after a lease edit, all draft-only. New `lib/Billing/InvoiceRecalc.php` (`InvoiceRecalc::recalc($invoiceId)`) is the single recompute both the editor and regenerate will share: rebuilds subtotal/discount/tax/total/balance from the line items using the invoice's FROZEN tax-rate + exemption snapshots (never live rates → can't silently re-tax a draft, D14/CRA), credit-aware subtotal, non-taxable lines untaxed, discount prorated, and invoice `tax_total` == Σ(line tax) so totals always reconcile. Caller owns the transaction + draft-only/permission/audit. **Verified:** `tests/_smoke_invoice_recalc.php` 24/24 hermetic (credit lines, non-taxable, GST exemption, flat + % discount, balance net of payment, line↔invoice reconciliation). No schema change. **Note found:** `createFromLease` is stateful (mutates `leases.precharge_balance` via Model B drawdown at draft creation), so regenerate must reverse-then-reapply that — handled in a later part; the manual editor (next) is side-effect-free. Nothing user-facing yet.
- S-LEASE-EDIT-DIRTY-ONLY + S-LEASE-EDIT-ACTIVE-UNLOCK SHIPPED (this commit) — **Two operator-reported Edit Lease fixes.** (1) **Don't clear untouched fields:** the form posted the whole model every save, and `update.php` writes any field present in the body, so form defaults (NULL→`''`, factor→`0.621371`, costs→`0.00`/`1.00`) silently changed fields the user never touched. Fixed with client dirty-tracking in `app/admin/leases/edit.php` — snapshot the server values in `init()` (`_initial`), and `submit()` POSTs only fields that differ from baseline + `id`/`updated_at` (no-change save = no-op redirect). update.php is already partial-update-safe. (2) **Active leases fully editable:** removed the blanket `ACTIVE_LEASE_DISTANCE_ONLY` server gate in `api/v1/leases/update.php`; the form's policy-locked fields (PO, dates, rate notes, min-billing-days, precharge, add-ons, notes) now render as inputs for all statuses, with the active-lease banner rewritten to warn "edits apply to future billing only — will not alter invoices already sent." Already-billed precharge/cartage stay frozen, ending mileage/hours stay close-owned, rates stay on the Amendments tab, completed/cancelled stay read-only. **Verified:** dirty-diff node test (6 cases); live authenticated pending lease (`_initial` 28/28, no-change payload = id+updated_at only, single change = 1 field); live active lease end-to-end (`internal_notes` saved successfully — no `ACTIVE_LEASE_DISTANCE_ONLY` — then restored, DB confirmed); rate-tier + optimistic-lock smokes 7/7. No schema change. **Needs operator `ff-deploy`.**
- S-MONTHLY-SHORT-FLAT-PICKER SHIPPED (this commit) — **The invoice month-picker now mirrors generation's spanning decision: a ≤1-month straddle (and any non-spanning cross-month lease) shows ONE billable segment instead of two.** Follow-up to S-MONTHLY-SHORT-FLAT (deferred to keep that money fix's blast radius minimal). **Problem:** S-MONTHLY-SHORT-FLAT made a ≤30-day monthly straddle bill ONE flat invoice (basis `monthly_short_flat` → `InvoiceGenerator.php:1916` treats it as non-spanning), but the picker source `api/v1/leases/billable_months.php` (`ff_billable_months`) called `HolisticLeaseEngine::segmentsFor()` UNCONDITIONALLY (a pure calendar-month walker), so it still showed TWO month rows (e.g. July + August) while generation wrote ONE flat invoice — billing month-by-month booked the full flat month (e.g. $425) to the first segment and $0 to the second (correct total, contradictory split), breaking the file's docblock contract that segments "match exactly what generation would write." (A latent analogous divergence existed for sub-month WEEKLY cross-month leases.) **Fix (1 file, `billable_months.php`):** after computing the extent E, classify the full-extent lease via `cumulativeCorrect(start, E, E, rates)` and call `segmentsFor()` ONLY when `basis === 'monthly_multi_month'` (the exact predicate `generateForLease` uses); otherwise present ONE `single_period` segment `[start, E]` (final when E is definitive) — exactly what `generateForLease`'s non-spanning path writes. Added `daily/weekly/monthly_rate` to the lease SELECT; gated the classification on `engine_version === 'holistic'` (legacy/mileage leases never fan out and the picker UI already hides for them) so they show ONE segment too. The in-order gate (`next_due_index`/`fully_billed`), per-segment status, and the `single_segment` generation flow are unchanged — they just iterate one segment in the non-spanning case. **Result:** a ≤1-month straddle shows ONE "Billing Month" row that bills ONE flat monthly invoice in one click; no contradictory $0 second month. **Tests:** runner **555/555**; `_smoke_invoice_month_picker` — added P10 (22-day Jul24→Aug14 straddle → ONE segment + ONE flat **$1,500** invoice) and P11 (9-day Jul28→Aug05 sub-month WEEKLY cross-month → ONE segment + ONE **$642.86** invoice); existing P2–P5 multi-month spanning unaffected (still 2 segments); P6 #2620 pre-existing seed-data fail (lease absent from this dev DB). No schema change. **Needs operator `ff-deploy`.**
- S-MONTHLY-SHORT-FLAT SHIPPED (this commit) — **A ≤1-month monthly-tier lease that STRADDLES a calendar-month boundary now bills the FLAT monthly rate, not the sum of two partial-month prorations.** Operator report: "invoice 171, 22 days charged total should have defaulted to the monthly rate." **Diagnosis (read-only prod):** lease MTTS73 (id 77), Jul 24 → Aug 14 2025 = 22 days (daily $40 / weekly $180 / monthly $425), closed into 2 drafts (INV-2026-00170 weekly $205.71 for Jul 24-31; INV-2026-00171 the close). The engine correctly entered the monthly regime (weeklyMath(22)=$565.71 > $425) but, with NO complete calendar month in the span, `sumSegments()` prorated both partials at monthly÷30 → 22 × $14.1667 = **$311.67**, undercutting the flat monthly. A 22-day lease wholly inside one month bills flat $425 (§4.1) — so the straddle got a silent calendar-position discount. This was the deliberate, tested R2 §4.2/§5 "commit longer, better per-day" behaviour (S-BILLING-HOLISTIC-GENERATE-FIX). **Operator confirmed (AskUserQuestion): flat monthly, always.** **Fix (`lib/Billing/HolisticLeaseEngine.php` `cumulativeCorrect()` monthly branch):** new `monthly_short_flat` case — monthly applies AND `total ≤ 30` days but NOT a single calendar month → return the FLAT `$monthly` (ignores `$through` like §4.1) instead of `sumSegments()`. In-month flat (`monthly_single_month`), >30-day spanning (`monthly_multi_month`), and the rate-driven regime crossover are UNCHANGED. Flat-cap gate is calendar-aware: `total ≤ 30 OR withinOneCalendarMonth(start, extentEnd)` (monthiversary = start + 1 calendar month, end-of-month clamped). The within-one-month arm flats a 31-day rolling month (operator: "30- and 31-day months are both one month"); the ≤30 arm stays to keep the charge monotonic across short February rolling months (a 29-day Feb straddle would otherwise prorate below the 28-day flat). 975-point sweep = zero inversions. The lone `basis` consumer (`InvoiceGenerator.php:1916`) treats `monthly_short_flat` as non-spanning → ONE invoice. **MTTS73 now:** cumulative $311.67 → **$425.00**; INV-2026-00171 base $105.96 → **$219.29**; total base = the monthly rate $425.00. **Tests:** runner **555/555** (updated the only 3 ≤30-day-straddle assertions — RR-020 $150→$500, ML-006 $350→$700, ML-007 $676.67→$700); `_stress_holistic_engine` 88/88; `_smoke_holistic_generate_fix` 46/46; `_smoke_invoice_month_picker` 46/46 (P6 #2620 pre-existing seed-data fail, identical on clean HEAD); `_smoke_invoice_dating` 28/28; `_smoke_lease_minimum_days` 36/36. **Adversarial verify (3-agent workflow): ZERO actionable findings.** No schema change. **Needs operator `ff-deploy` + MTTS73 remediation (F39).**

- S-LEASE-CLOSE-ACTUAL-DATE SHIPPED (this commit) — **Customer now sees the ACTUAL lease dates on a closeout invoice when the return-day-not-billed time-of-day rule trims the last day** (the trim stays a billing calc, not a shorter rental). Before: a morning return (≤ pickup time + grace) dropped the final billed day and the invoice showed the trimmed extent (e.g. Jul 24) instead of the real return (Jul 25). Now the base-rental line reads `"Base rental: Jul 18 – Jul 25 · billed 7 days (return day not charged — returned by pickup time)"` and the portal Period row/column show the actual return date — while `billing_period_end` stays the trimmed extent (the S-CLOSE-OVERSHOOT invariant forbids billing past the extent, so the column can't move; display-layer fix only). No schema change.
- S-FAVICON-EXTEND SHIPPED (this commit) — **Follow-up to S-FAVICON-SINGLE-LINK: extends the single-favicon helper + prod asset-path fix to every remaining `<head>`.** (A) The auth/account/public pages now honor the uploaded favicon via `ff_favicon_tags()`: `app/auth/{login,forgot_password,reset_password,accept_invite,mfa_required,mfa_challenge}.php`, `app/portal/auth/{login,forgot_password,reset_password}.php`, `app/admin/credit-application.php` (the two login pages migrated from a working if/else `<link>` to the helper, keeping the `$loginFavicon`/`$loginFaviconUrl` computations that feed the live brand-preview JS; `app/account/mfa_setup.php` doesn't exist — skipped). (B) Error pages had a prod asset 404: `app/errors/{403,404,500,maintenance}.php` built asset URLs with `base_url()`/`$_baseUrl` = `APP_URL . FF_BASE_PATH` → `/fleetforge/assets/…`, which 404s on prod (verified: `/assets/css/app.css` 200 vs `/fleetforge/assets/css/app.css` 404) — so both the favicon AND the stylesheet were broken there. Fixed: 403/404 use `asset_url()` for css+favicon (nav links keep `base_url()`); 500/maintenance (helper-free by design) get a new primitive `$_assetBase = rtrim(APP_URL,'/')` for css+favicon while `$_baseUrl` still drives in-app links. Error pages keep the default favicon (no DB lookup) for robustness. **Verified:** php -l ×14 clean; per-file sweep (1 favicon emit each, no `base_url('assets…` left); Herd preview — admin login = 1 icon link (helper), 404 page = 1 icon link + css both at the root `/assets/…` path. Needs operator `ff-deploy`.
- S-BTN-OUTLINE-WARNING-DARK SHIPPED (this commit) — **The "Reopen Lease" button on the lease detail page was unreadable (dark-on-dark) in dark mode** (operator screenshot — near-black text next to a correctly-styled blue "Generate Invoice"). **Root cause (CSS, not markup):** the button is `class="btn btn-outline-warning"` (`app/admin/leases/show.php:277`) but **`.btn-outline-warning` was never defined** in `public/assets/css/app.css`. The global `button {}` reset (app.css:196) sets `background:none; border:none` but never sets `color`, so a `.btn` with no colour variant falls through to the UA system colour `buttontext` (black), which the CSS-variable dark theme never adapts. **Fix (CSS-only, 1 file, 2 changes):** (1) defined `.btn-outline-warning` + `:hover` mirroring `.btn-outline-danger`, using `--color-warning-text` (not raw `--color-warning` — bare amber is too low-contrast as text): `#facc15` on dark, `#b45309` on light, both WCAG-AA; (2) hardened base `.btn` with `color: var(--text-primary)` as a fallback so ANY variant-less/undefined-variant `.btn` is readable instead of black (safe — every colour variant is defined after `.btn` and overrides it; zero variant-less `.btn` render today, so it's a pure guardrail). **"Every other button with the same error":** an exhaustive full-tag scan (merging `class` + Alpine `:class` + inline `style`) over every `<button>`/`<a>`/`<input>` in all php/html/js under `app/`+`public/` found `btn-outline-warning` was the ONLY genuinely-broken button — false positives (`ff-bulk-btn-*`/`tab-btn--*`/`perm-bulk-btn--*`/`chat-action-btn--*` own bases, `btn-tab` inline `:style`, `error.php` self-scoped, ~20 `btn btn-sm` toggles with sibling Alpine `:class`) all confirmed fine. **Verified** against the real stylesheet via temp dark+light preview harnesses: `.btn-outline-warning` computes `rgb(250,204,21)`/#facc15 (dark) and `rgb(180,83,9)`/#b45309 (light); a control `btn btn-sm` resolved to readable `--text-primary` instead of black; `btn-primary`/`btn-secondary` unchanged (no regression); before/after screenshots both themes; harnesses removed. No JS/markup/schema change; cache-bust automatic via `FF_ASSET_VERSION` (git HEAD). **Needs operator `ff-deploy` to take effect on prod.**
- S-FAVICON-SINGLE-LINK SHIPPED (this commit) — **A favicon uploaded via Settings → Design didn't update on production.** Read-only prod diagnosis proved the backend is fine: prod is on S3, the upload saved correctly (`brand.favicon_path`, audit-logged), `StorageClient::exists()`=YES (3981 bytes), and the header emits a valid pre-signed S3 URL that GETs 200. **Root cause:** the admin app `<head>` (`includes/header.php`) emitted TWO `<link rel="icon">` — the static SVG default (always) AND the uploaded PNG override — and Chrome/Firefox prefer an SVG icon when one is declared regardless of DOM order, so the SVG won and the upload was ignored (browser favicon caching compounded it). The portal app + legal heads also hardcoded the SVG and never read the setting. **Fix:** new shared helper `ff_favicon_tags()` (`includes/functions.php`) emits exactly ONE icon link (custom favicon if `brand.favicon_path` set, else cache-busted SVG default; type from key ext; exception-safe). Wired into the admin header (the fix), the portal app header, and the legal header. Also `StorageClient::uploadS3()` now sets `ContentType` (sniffed) so future uploads aren't `application/octet-stream`. **Verified:** php -l ×5 clean; helper unit-tested (both branches); `/legal/terms` preview emits exactly 1 icon link; prod login curl already shows the PNG. Auth-helper/error pages still hardcode the SVG (error pages' `base_url()` favicon also 404s on prod) — offered as a follow-up. Needs operator `ff-deploy` + a hard refresh (favicon cache). Unrelated `.btn` app.css drift left out of the commit per the master-sync rule.
- S-NOTIF-LIGHT-HOVER SHIPPED (this commit) — **Fixed the notification dropdown rendering a near-black, unreadable box on the hovered unread row in DAY (light) mode** (operator screenshot). **Root cause was a light-theme palette gap, not notification-specific:** `public/assets/css/app.css` defined `--bg-muted` / `--bg-hover` only under `[data-theme="dark"]`, so every light-mode rule using `var(--bg-muted|--bg-hover, <dark literal>)` fell through to a near-black literal — most visibly `.notif-item--unread:hover → var(--bg-hover, #2a2a2a)` on the row under the cursor (with light-mode dark text). The same gap silently broke ~50 other light-mode hover surfaces (portal nav/tables, chat/messenger lists, email chips, pickers — dark or no-hover in light mode). **Fix (2 lines):** added `--bg-muted: #f1f5f9` (slate-100) and `--bg-hover: #e2e8f0` (slate-200) to the `[data-theme="light"]` block, mirroring the dark-theme token relationship; dark mode untouched (its tokens take precedence), resting unread stays peach. **Verified** against the real stylesheet in the preview (light mode): computed `--bg-hover` = `#e2e8f0`, hovered unread row = `rgb(226,232,240)` readable gray + dark text + orange unread border; before/after screenshot; no console errors. CSS-only; no schema/JS/markup change; cache-bust automatic via `FF_ASSET_VERSION` (git HEAD hash).
- S-SAMSARA-OFFLINE-NOTIF-OFF SHIPPED (this commit) — **Stopped the Samsara "device offline" notification flood** (operator: "I get 100s every day"). `cron/samsara_sync.php` fired a grouped "N units offline" in-app notification for ANY linked unit whose Samsara check-in was >8h stale, with no trailer exclusion — but the fleet is mostly **unpowered trailers** that normally go quiet for 8h+ while parked, so the alert was near-pure noise (compounded by a 6h per-unit re-fire and fan-out to every `equipment:view` user + super_admins). Per operator decision (AskUserQuestion), the device-offline notification (`samsara.not_connected`) is now **removed entirely** for both vehicles and trailers; **battery-critical/low alerts kept**. Three surgical edits to the cron (drop the `$pendingAlerts` key, the per-unit accumulation block, and the grouped-dispatch `$alertMeta` entry) + removed the now-dead offline-alert timezone resolution. **GPS/mileage sync, breadcrumbs, and `samsara_last_connected_at` storage untouched** — offline status is still shown on the Samsara fleet dashboard + tracking page; only the push-notification is gone. `php -l` clean; grep confirms zero remaining generators. No schema change. **Needs operator `ff-deploy` to take effect on prod.**
- S-CLOSE-SWEEP-WASH-DEFAULT-0 SHIPPED (this commit) — **Sweep + wash closeout charges now default to 0 (blank) at lease close**, instead of pre-filling $30 / $120. The close modal pre-filled `sweep_amount` / `wash_amount` from the (UI-less) `lease.{sweep,wash}_charge_default` settings; now they default to blank like `fuel_gallons`, so closeout charges are opt-in (the operator types an amount to bill). `close.php`'s `$svcDec()` already treats blank/0 as "no charge", so no server change was needed. `app/admin/leases/show.php` only; no schema change.
- S-DB-CONNECT-RETRY SHIPPED (this commit) — **Killed the recurring prod Sentry issue `PDOException: SQLSTATE[HY000] [2002] Connection refused` (`includes/db.php`).** (Sentry MCP was unreachable — `net::ERR_FAILED` — so root-caused from the prod nginx + MySQL + apt logs, which is what the SDK captures.) **Root cause:** `unattended-upgrades` applied a `mysql-server` security update at 06:11 2026-06-23 (also 2026-06-03) and restarted mysqld for ~10s; `db_pdo()` made a single connect attempt, so every request in the window — chiefly the high-frequency unread-count pollers — threw an unhandled PDOException → 500 + Sentry (3 restart windows ≈ the "30 users / 30d" count). **Fix:** new `db_connect_with_retry()` (bounded exponential backoff, ~1.4s worst case) + `db_is_transient_connect_error()` classifier in `includes/db.php` — retries ONLY transient connect codes (2002/2003/2006/2013/1040/1053) and fails fast on permanent ones (1045/1049); `db_pdo()` routes through it, happy path unchanged. **Test:** NEW `_smoke_db_connect_retry.php` 4/4 (classifier; transient-retries-with-backoff; permanent-fails-fast; happy path); mileage smoke 7/7 regression-clean. **Operator follow-up F37** (🟢 DEFERRED — optionally pin/exclude the MySQL auto-upgrade window; app retry already mitigates). No schema change.
- S-CLOSE-MANUAL-MILEAGE-BRIDGE SHIPPED (this commit) — **Follow-up to S-MILEAGE-MODE-OFF-WARN: makes manual mileage actually BILL, not just warn.** Closed the two remaining "manual mileage didn't show up" gaps from INV-2026-00150. (b) **The bridge** — the close form's "Actual Mileage (for billing)" field (`leases.mileage_at_end`) now actually bills on modern manual leases (it previously fed ONLY the legacy `mileage_at_start`+`mileage_rate` overage path, so SAMSARA-3-era manual leases silently dropped it). New pure helper `ff_close_manual_mileage_bridge_line()` (in `_close_reconciliation.php`) builds one `mileage_usage` line = distance × `mileage_rate_km` (km-canonical; miles→km converted), appended via the proven `$extraLines` carrier so it bills on the advance/partial_end/adjustment invoice alike. FOUR guards make double-billing impossible (manual-only; `mileage_at_start` NULL; `odoAtClose` NULL; no prior mileage line). (a) **The warning** — a manual lease with a rate that closes with no billable mileage now emits a non-blocking `[FLEETFORGE_BILLING_WARNING]` (audit + Sentry + `mileage_warning` response) and a close-form pre-submit confirm. **Verified:** `_smoke_lease_mileage_mode.php` 7/7 (T6 helper gating incl. 175 km × $0.06 = $10.50 + miles conversion; T7 line bills $36.00 via the real `createFromLease`, exactly one mileage line); advance_close_mileage / bulk_close_tail / billing_invariants I1–I10 / refund_state_machine all green. **Adversarial 3-lens review (double-billing / missed-carrier / warning+regression) → SHIP, zero high/critical**; lone low finding (warning miss on never-invoiced precharge lease) fixed inline. 4 files; no schema change.
- S-MILEAGE-MODE-OFF-WARN SHIPPED (this commit) — **Manual mileage silently not billed: a lease with `mileage_rate_km>0` but `mileage_tracking_mode='off'` bills $0 mileage with no signal.** Root-caused from prod invoice INV-2026-00150 (lease 74 MTTS68): created with mode='off' (the column DEFAULT) despite a $0.06/km rate + an operator-entered ending reading; `'off'` is a hard kill-switch in `InvoiceGenerator` (nulls the odometer at :594-597, and the `mileage_usage` gate requires `mode !== 'off'` at :765), so no mileage line can ever emit. Contributing: "175" landed in the legacy int `mileage_at_end` (biller reads only the decimal `odometer_*_km`) and `odometer_start_km` was NULL. **Guardrails added (3 files + smoke):** (1) `lib/Billing/InvoiceGenerator.php` — non-blocking billing WARNING (Sentry `warning` + `audit_log [FLEETFORGE_BILLING_WARNING]`) when mode='off' AND mileage_rate_km>0 on a normal billing type; UNIVERSAL net firing on every path (close / manual invoice / monthly cron), mirrors the existing D-C soft-warning pattern, never throws. (2) `app/admin/leases/create.php` — rate-aware inline `alert-danger` + escalated `submit()` confirm naming the configured rate. (3) `app/admin/leases/edit.php` — same rate-aware inline alert + submit confirm (rate read-only there → server-gated on persisted rate). Smoke `tests/_smoke_lease_mileage_mode.php` T5 added (off+rate warns, manual doesn't) — 5/5 pass against real schema. Operator remediation: INV-2026-00150 is still a DRAFT → add one `175 km × $0.06 = $10.50` line (operator confirmed 175 = distance driven). php -l clean (3 files); doc-freshness green. No schema change.

- S-UNIT-MILEAGE-TILE-SAMSARA SHIPPED — **Operator-noticed: the "Mileage" hero tile showed "0 mi" on Samsara-tracked trailer 40TR1351 despite a real 4,136.64 km GPS odometer.** ROOT CAUSE: the tile (app/admin/equipment/show.php hero cards) rendered only `equipment_units.mileage` (manual integer + hard-coded "mi") and never consulted `samsara_odometer_km` (which only appeared in the Samsara Live Tracking → Odometer cell). - S-UNIT-DISTANCE-RENDER-FIX SHIPPED — **Operator-caught: clicking "Calculate" in the Distance Travelled section rendered nothing for any unit (e.g. 40TR1351), even though the endpoint returned 200 and `distResult.distance` was correctly set.** ROOT CAUSE (Alpine x-if multi-root): `<template x-if="distResult">` in app/admin/equipment/show.php wrapped THREE sibling branch `<template>`s; Alpine v3 x-if renders only a SINGLE root element, so it kept only the first branch (`distResult.linked === false`, false for a successful result) and silently dropped the distance-display branch — the number had no element to render into. Pre-existing defect from S-UNIT-DISTANCE-SECTION; surfaced widely after S-UNIT-DISTANCE-UNIVERSAL made the calculator visible on all unit types. (The prior universal-fix "render-verify" used a standalone `x-html` harness that bypassed the real nested-`<template x-if>` structure, so it could not catch this — harness must exercise the real markup.) FIX (UI only): wrapped the three branch templates in a single root `<div>`. VERIFIED in the real authenticated browser (manager session on the dev server): unit 40TR1351 → Last 30 days → Calculate → renders **503.83 km** with GPS badge + reading window + warnings. SC1 php -l clean; doc_freshness 29/29. View-layer only — ff-deploy, no migration. Commit: `<this commit>` (show.php + 2 docs).
- S-UNIT-DISTANCE-UNIVERSAL SHIPPED — **Distance Travelled section now appears on EVERY equipment/unit type + shows a note when no GPS is linked.** Bug reported as "doesn't show on reefer units"; ROOT CAUSE was linkage, not type: the section was wrapped in `<template x-if="unit.samsara_vehicle_id">` (app/admin/equipment/show.php), hiding it for ANY unlinked unit. No type/category gate exists anywhere near it, and it already lived in the shared Overview layout. It only *looked* type-specific because reefer (0/5), flatbed (0/4), step_deck (0/3) and other (0/5) are 100% unlinked in data (vs dry_van 173/181 linked). - S-CVI-OVERDUE-SIGN-FIX SHIPPED — **A CVI expiry in the FUTURE (e.g. Aug 2026) showed "76 days overdue" + red on the unit page.** ROOT CAUSE: `app/admin/equipment/show.php` PHP `daysUntil()` computed `$date->diff(today)`, which inverts for future dates → returned a NEGATIVE day count for future expiries → the `< 0 ? overdue` render/colour mislabelled them. Sign was reversed vs the page's own correct JS twin. FIX (show.php only): `daysUntil()` anchors `today->$date` (positive = future "expires in N days", negative = past "overdue N days", 0 = "expires today"); NEW `complianceDelta()` PHP + JS twins render those labels in both the stat card and the Documents-tab table. 30-day colour thresholds unchanged (future >30d now teal, was red). Only this one PHP helper was wrong (verified repo-wide; other "days overdue" renders are invoice-aging, different domain).
