throws if ZipArchive missing (caught by close()). `reverse` is super_admin-only — JournalEntryService::reverse() on closing JE + UPDATE periods back to 'closed' (NOT 'open') + flips closure status='reversed'. **Part B** 2 checklist endpoints: GET seeds canonical 17 items idempotently per fiscal_year via UNIQUE(year, item_key); POST toggles is_complete with completed_by/_at stamping. **Part C** 4 year-end APIs: preflight (passes is_super_admin role flag); close (POST — non-super_admin 403'd on override flag; specific error codes ALREADY_CLOSED 409 / PREFLIGHT_FAILED 422 / OVERRIDE_REQUIRED 422 / CONFIG_INCOMPLETE 422 / CLOSING_JE_UNBALANCED 500); reverse (POST, super_admin only, requires reason); package_download (verifies SHA-256 hash before streaming, audit-logs download, Content-Disposition: attachment). **Part D** single-page admin console: fiscal-year dropdown + two-column grid (Checklist with progress bar + 17 toggleable items; Pre-Flight Checks with per-check pass/warn/fail icons + AR override checkbox shown only when super_admin + AR drift > $1) + Close button gated on canClose() (checklist 100% + preflight pass + override acknowledged if needed) + confirmation modal + post-close result card with download button + prior-closures table with download + super_admin Reverse button per closed row. Nav updates in both accounting-nav.php (calendar-days icon top-level) + config/navigation.php (sidebar child with match_prefix). **Live smoke** preflight(2025, false) reports ar_drift=$-49,677.04 is_blocking=YES (non-super_admin), ap_drift=$37,486.40 is_blocking=YES, checklist_complete=12/17 incomplete is_blocking=YES, can_proceed=NO; preflight(2025, true) correctly demotes AR to is_warning=YES (super_admin soft gate per D-S037-YE-1) while keeping AP drift + incomplete checklist hard-blocking. Did NOT execute close() in smoke (would mutate production-tier data — operator authorization required for actual close). **3 DECISIONS locked**: D-S037-YE-1 (AR soft gate via super_admin override flag — non-super_admin remains hard-blocked, override documented in audit_log), D-S037-YE-2 (year-end package scoped to AVAILABLE reports — 8 PDFs + 4 placeholders for pending Phase C reports), D-S037-YE-3 (checklist auto-seeds 17 canonical items on first GET, idempotent via UNIQUE constraint). **PREDEPLOY items added** under D — AWS infrastructure: D-YE-1 (refactor generatePackage() + package_download.php to use StorageClient::upload()/url() before first production year-end close — currently writes to FF_ROOT/storage which won't survive server rebuild) + D-YE-2 (verify ZipArchive PHP extension on prod via `php -m | grep -i zip`). **Touched files (12):** YearEndService.php + 7 API endpoints + admin page + 2 nav files + migration + DATABASE_MASTER.sql sync. **PHP lint clean** on all 10 .php files. **D131 6/6 PASS** pre-commit: PARITY OK + INVARIANTS OK I1-I10 + samsara 16/16 + model_b_lifecycle 20/20 + doc_freshness 17/17 + migrate 29/0/0.

AND status='posted' check. **Part B** `cron/accounting_fx_revaluation.php` (NEW): advisory-locked, finds most-recent-closed-period for prior month, skips if already posted, fetches rate via service, audit-logs; exits 0 silently when disabled / no closed period / already-posted (NOT errors). **Part C** 5 API endpoints under `api/v1/accounting/fx-revaluations/`: index (paginated list with period+JE joins, year+status filters), show (header + JE + lines), preview (GET with manual_rate override; surfaces rate-fetch errors as FX_RATE_UNAVAILABLE 422 so UI prompts for manual rate), post (requires confirm=1 safety flag; specific error codes ALREADY_POSTED 409 / FX_DISABLED 422 / CONFIG_INCOMPLETE 422), reverse. **4 settings** INSERTed idempotently: accounting.fx_revaluation_enabled='0' (off by default — operator opts in), accounting.fx_rate_source='bank_of_canada', accounting.fx_revaluation_account_id='' (engine falls back to existing fx_gain_account_id/fx_loss_account_id pre-seed), accounting.fx_manual_rate=''. **Part D** `app/admin/accounting/fx-revaluations/index.php` (NEW): single-page console — Run panel (closed-period dropdown + rate source display + manual override input + Preview button + Confirm checkbox + Post button) showing preview table with USD/CAD-book/CAD-revalued/delta per account + footer total; History table with Period/Run-At/Rate/Gain-Loss/JE# (linked)/Status/Reverse-action; yellow banner if disabled; Alpine x-data factory with preview/post/reverse/loadHistory + currency formatter. **Nav updates** in both accounting-nav.php (top-level item with currency-dollar icon) + config/navigation.php (sidebar child with match_prefix). **End-to-end smoke test** against live DB: preview(period 15, '1.365') returns 1 USD account (Cash USD code 1020) with $0 balance per STOP CONDITION = correct; post() inserts revaluation row with status='posted', je_id=NULL (zero delta — no JE needed); duplicate post() correctly rejected with "Revaluation already posted. ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE` so saves self-heal regardless of seed state. **Fix 2 (`includes/sidebar.php`):** sidebar logo render previously read `settings_get('company.logo_url')` — a key NEVER written anywhere — instead of `brand.logo_path` (the StorageClient key actually written by the Design tab). Side-effect: S-DESIGN-SETTINGS-FOOTER-LOGIN seed migration applied during this fix via `bin/migrate.php --apply` (migration 25/26 now in `schema_migrations`).

All rights reserved.` with `Avi Technologies` fallback), `docs/FLEETFORGE_DESIGN_DETAILS.md` (+50 lines new section 2). 19 settings rows seeded: brand.{primary_color, primary_hover, primary_light, logo_path, favicon_path}, defaults.{theme, density, font_size, rows_per_page}, regional.{date_format, time_format, currency_symbol, timezone, distance_unit}, pdf.{invoice_footer_text, show_logo, accent_color}, ui.{sidebar_collapsed_default, session_timeout_minutes}. - S-BILLING-HOLISTIC-ENGINE SHIPPED (single-commit feature arc — see PROGRESS.md SESSION LOG row) — replaces ProRateCalculator THE LAW (period-independent) with `lib/Billing/HolisticLeaseEngine.php` running-reconciliation engine for new leases. **Boss's bug fixed:** old engine billed each period in isolation, so a lease crossing tier boundaries (e.g. 4-day partial March + 30-day full April at $50/$350/$700) overcharged by $106.67 (billed $900 vs correct $793.33). **Algorithm:** every invoice answers — (1) inclusive day count start→period_end; (2) tier via same THE LAW thresholds (1-5 daily / 6-7 weekly_flat / 8-29 weekly_math capped at monthly / 30+ monthly_math); (3) cumulative_correct from tier formula; (4) already_billed = SUM(prior non-void base_rental net of reconciliation credits); (5) delta = cumulative − already. Positive → `base_rental` line; zero → no line; negative → `base_rental_reconciliation_credit` (POSITIVE amount + is_credit=1 per K-16, matches mileage_drawdown_credit shape). Activation invoices apply spec §17 "whichever pays more" (HIGHER of current-tier or monthly-prorated) — engine confirms activation against ground truth so the caller's billing_type guess can't fire it on Invoice 2+. **6 migrations** (0100-0105) all idempotent (ff_add_column_if_missing for ADDs; append-at-end ENUM extensions per D126; INSERT IGNORE for the settings flag): invoice audit columns (`total_days_at_period_end` + `cumulative_correct_amount` + `already_billed_before_this`, NULL on period_independent rows), invoice_line_items.item_type +1 (`base_rental_reconciliation_credit`, ENUM 16→17), credit_notes.source +1 (`base_rental_reconciliation_overflow`, 8→9), leases.engine_version ENUM with DEFAULT='holistic', lock 23 active/pending leases to 'period_independent' (8 closed leases got 'holistic' default harmlessly), settings flag `billing.engine_version=holistic` is_public=0 (paper spec said is_editable — column doesn't exist on live; used is_public). **InvoiceGenerator dispatch** branches on `lease.engine_version`: holistic path calls `calculateForInvoice()`, emits engine's chosen line type, populates the three audit columns, writes audit_log entity_type='invoice_holistic_reconciliation' with full audit_meta JSON. Holistic path uses FOR UPDATE on lease row (D20) for the already_billed read. Added `mapTierToRateMethod()` helper to collapse engine's richer tier vocabulary to the invoices.rate_method_used ENUM domain. **Overflow handler extended (spec §19.2):** the existing S-FIX-2 Bug #3 cap mechanism now accepts EITHER mileage_credit OR base_rental_reconciliation_credit (priority mileage_credit-first to preserve pre-existing byte-for-byte behavior); credit_notes.source branches accordingly. **Tests `tests/_stress_holistic_engine.php`** — 88/88 pass. Unit: every Appendix A tier boundary; whicheverPaysMore A/B/tie; inclusiveDays leap years + year boundaries + inverted-range defense. Integration: boss's exact example ($200 + $593.33 = $793.33 ✓), day 5/6/7/8/14 closes (tier crossings), leap year Feb 1-29 (29d = $700 capped), 365-day annual lease, 6-month sequence ($3,220 cumulative), negative-delta scenario with overflow → credit_notes row, holistic audit_log row present. Regression: period_independent still uses ProRateCalculator + never emits reconciliation_credit + never populates audit columns. **Adjacent suites pass:** _stress_billing_rate_exception 11/11, _stress_invoice_generator_precharge 6/6, _stress_drawdown_emit 6/6, _smoke_billing_invariants I1-I10 OK, _smoke_master_schema_parity OK after audit-column repositioning in master to match ALTER TABLE ADD COLUMN's at-end placement. **Migrate:** dry-run 6 pending (stop condition #6 met), apply 6/6 in 675ms, verify 25 ok / 0 drift / 0 missing. **Spec §35.7 precision band:** day 65/365/366 differ from Appendix A's display values by $0.02 because spec uses pre-rounded $23.33 × N while engine uses bcdiv($monthly,'30',6) × N per spec §7.2 pseudocode (matches ProRateCalculator). Boss-approved 2-cent tolerance in §35.7. **Deviations:** (1) spec §30.1 marked `whicheverPaysMore` private; exposed public for unit-testability — no behavior change; (2) §29.4 settings used is_editable which doesn't exist; used is_public per actual schema; (3) §7.3 SUM extended to include base_rental_reconciliation_credit with signed semantics so already_billed never double-counts a prior reconciliation. **Open Questions §35 resolved at build:** §35.1 confirmed (WPM activation-only); §35.2 Option A (lock existing); §35.3 activation = already_billed===0 ground truth; §35.5 rate-change-on-active prohibited at policy layer (not engine-enforced this session); §35.6 same-day = 1 day verified; §35.7 2-cent tolerance accepted. **NOT touched:** ProRateCalculator (kept for period_independent leases; deprecation comes when all old-engine leases close — could take months per spec §32); TaxCalculator (handles negatives via bcmul sign-propagation — confirmed); api/v1/leases/create.php (D132 backstop already enforces zero-rate-with-billable-days; no new validation needed per spec §31.3); api/v1/leases/close.php (calls InvoiceGenerator which dispatches automatically based on lease.engine_version). PHP lint clean on all new + modified files.
- S-STORAGE-DOCS-GITIGNORE SHIPPED (single-commit code arc d62d469 + docs commit — see PROGRESS.md SESSION LOG row) — forward-looking remediation of an 8-file customer-PII leak class that had been silently committable since the S022 Documents Module shipped 2026-04-05. **Pre-flight scan:** `git ls-files storage/documents/` returned 8 PDFs (1 customer/ + 6 equipment_unit/ + 1 lease/), all introduced in one earlier commit `19d64676 feat: VALID-1 — Form Validation Hardening` — `git log --diff-filter=A -- storage/documents/` confirmed no fixture intent (no seed/test/fixture commit ever added files under this path; the adding commit is a form-validation feature that incidentally swept in upload artifacts via `git add .`). **Root cause:** `.gitignore` covered `storage/uploads/` + `storage/generated/` + `storage/maintenance.flag` from the storage tree but NOT `storage/documents/`, so any `git add .` after a documents upload silently tracked the customer-PII PDF that `api/v1/documents/upload.php:137` writes via `StorageClient::store()`. **Fix:** `.gitignore` adds `storage/documents/` after the existing `storage/generated/` entry; `git rm --cached` on all 8 PDFs removes from index but LEAVES files on disk so the app keeps serving them via `api/v1/documents/serve.php` (signed URL chain via StorageClient per upload.php Trap 7). Post-rm: `git ls-files storage/documents/` → 0; on-disk `ls` confirms all 8 PDFs (~715KB each) still present at their writeroot paths. Pre-push: `git rev-list --left-right --count origin/main...HEAD` confirmed clean fast-forward `0\t1` before `git push origin HEAD:main` completed `34730ff..d62d469`. **NOT in scope** — requires separate operator sign-off — rewriting git history to purge the PDFs from past commits (`git filter-branch` / `bfg-repo-cleaner`); the 8 PDFs remain reachable via commit 19d64676 and every subsequent tree until that decision is made. **Operator action post-pull:** if any clone of this repo has these 8 PDFs on disk locally (vs reading from S3 via StorageClient) and is serving them from there, pulling main past d62d469 will REMOVE them from that working tree — git treats the rm --cached commit as a tree-level deletion regardless of `.gitignore`. The dev box where this commit was made is safe (rm --cached preserved the files there by construction). PHP not touched; schema not touched; no migrations; pure index/gitignore hygiene.
- S-RATELIMITER-TZ-FIX SHIPPED (single-commit arc — see PROGRESS.md SESSION LOG row) — fixed a clock-skew bug in `lib/Security/RateLimiter.php` that made `threshold > 0` settings unusable (and made `threshold = 0` accidentally block on attempt 2 instead of disabling the limiter) on any system where MySQL's session timezone differs from PHP's `date_default_timezone_get()`. **Surfaced via operator triage:** "Too many sign-in attempts from your network" lockout that wouldn't clear even with all rate-limit settings set to 0. Root cause was `strtotime((string) $row['window_start'])` parsing a UTC datetime string as Vancouver-local, shifting it ~7h into the future and making `($now - $windowStartTs)` always negative — the "window expired" branch never fired, so every attempt past the first hit the increment-and-block path. Same incident on the write side via `date('Y-m-d H:i:s', ...)` emitting Vancouver-local strings into a UTC column. Fix moved all timestamp comparisons into Unix-epoch integer space: SELECT uses `UNIX_TIMESTAMP(window_start)` + `UNIX_TIMESTAMP(blocked_until)` (returns NULL for NULL, integer otherwise); write uses `FROM_UNIXTIME(?)` (NULL passes through). No `strtotime()` or `date()` formatting remain in the timestamp paths. **New regression test `tests/_stress_rate_limiter_tz.php`** (7 cases / 14 assertions covering first-attempt creation, in-window increment, threshold-cross block, block honored, backdated `window_start` → reset (T5 — the specific assertion the bug killed pre-fix), backdated `blocked_until` → ignored, reset() clears row); 14/14 PASS. Verified live with the operator's current `threshold=0/window=0/block=0` settings — 5 sequential `check()` calls all return `allowed=true` (correct "limiter disabled" semantics; pre-fix it blocked on attempt 2). PHP syntax clean. Schema not touched; call sites in login.php / mfa_challenge.php / forgot_password.php / AI endpoints not touched (signature unchanged). Other `strtotime(<DB datetime string>)` sites across the codebase may have the same bug class and are queued as a possible follow-up audit if symptoms recur.
- S-DOC-UPLOAD-ENTITY-TYPE-FIX SHIPPED (single-commit arc — see PROGRESS.md SESSION LOG row) — defensive refactor of the documents/upload modal contract across 4 entry points after operator reported a 422 "entity_type must be one of:" from Customer detail → Documents tab → Upload Document. **Pre-flight finding (surfaced via AskUserQuestion per `feedback_trust_file_over_prompt.md`):** the bug as stated was not reproducible — all 4 existing upload paths (`app/admin/customers/show.php`, `app/admin/leases/show.php`, `app/admin/equipment/show.php`, `app/admin/documents/index.php`) already appended `entity_type` + `entity_id` to FormData, and the prompt's "5 entry points reuse a shared modal" framing did not match the file (each show page has its own inline Alpine modal — no shared `includes/partials/` upload modal exists; inspections/show.php + damage_claims/show.php have no documents upload UI at all — only photo uploads to entirely different endpoints `api/v1/inspections/photos/upload.php` + `api/v1/damage_claims/upload_photo.php`). Operator authorized the defensive refactor anyway. **Refactor:** `openDocUploadModal()` / `openLeaseDocModal()` / `openUploadModal()` now take `(entityType, entityId)` from the caller (equipment's preserves its `(docType, docLabel, …)` signature with the new args appended); modal state carries `entity_type` + `entity_id`; submit handlers refuse to post if either is missing (inline `'Cannot upload — missing context.'`) and read from state rather than hardcoded PHP-rendered constants; submit buttons' `:disabled` predicates extended to also check both fields. Failure mode flipped from "post empty entity_type → 422 from server" to "show inline error in modal + button disabled" — server never reached. Server-side defense in `api/v1/documents/upload.php` (allowlist L44-L49 + entity_id required L51-L54 + entity-exists check L80-L94) intact and not touched. Inspections + damage_claims pages NOT touched — adding new Documents tabs there would be a feature, not a fix. PHP syntax clean across all 5 files (`php -l` ran on the 4 view files + upload.php); on-disk grep of openers + state fields + FormData appends + disabled predicates confirms each entry point's contract holds.
